Find and remove linesegarray elements\n    for elem in list(paragraph):\n        if elem.tag == f'{{{ns[\"hp\"]}}}linesegarray':\n            paragraph.remove(elem)\n\ndef merge_and_replace_in_paragraph(paragraph, data, ns):\n    \"\"\"\n    Process a paragraph: merge text runs, replace placeholders, remove layout cache if changed.\n    Returns True if paragraph was modified.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Get all text runs in this paragraph\n    runs = find_text_runs(paragraph, ns)\n\n    if not runs:\n        return False\n\n    # Concatenate all text content from runs\n    full_text = ''.join(run.text or '' for run in runs)\n\n    # Check if any placeholders exist in this paragraph\n    placeholder_pattern = r'\\{\\{[^}]+\\}\\}'\n    placeholders = re.findall(placeholder_pattern, full_text)\n\n    if not placeholders:\n        return False\n\n    # Perform replacements\n    new_text = full_text\n    for key, value in data.items():\n        placeholder = f'{{{{{key}}}}}'\n        if placeholder in new_text:\n            # Convert value to string if needed\n            str_value = str(value) if not isinstance(value, str) else value\n            new_text = new_text.replace(placeholder, str_value)\n\n    # Clear all but the first run\n    first_run = runs[0]\n    for run in runs[1:]:\n        # Remove the run element from its parent\n        parent = find_parent(paragraph, run)\n        if parent is not None:\n            parent.remove(run)\n\n    # Set the merged and replaced text in the first run\n    first_run.text = new_text\n\n    # Remove layout cache elements\n    remove_linesegarray(paragraph, ns)\n\n    return True\n\ndef find_parent(root, child):\n    \"\"\"Find the parent of a child element.\"\"\"\n    for parent in root.iter():\n        for c in parent:\n            if c is child:\n                return parent\n    return None\n\ndef process_xml_content(xml_content, data):\n    \"\"\"Process XML content and replace placeholders.\"\"\"\n    # Parse XML\n    root = ET.fromstring(xml_content)\n\n    # Find all paragraphs\n    ns = NAMESPACES\n    paragraphs = []\n    for elem in root.iter():\n        if elem.tag == f'{{{ns[\"hp\"]}}}p':\n            paragraphs.append(elem)\n\n    # Process each paragraph\n    modified = False\n    for para in paragraphs:\n        if merge_and_replace_in_paragraph(para, data, ns):\n            modified = True\n\n    # Convert back to string\n    return ET.tostring(root, encoding='unicode')\n\ndef process_hwpx():\n    \"\"\"Main processing function.\"\"\"\n    # Load data\n    data = load_json_data(JSON_PATH)\n    print(f\"Loaded data with keys: {list(data.keys())}\")\n\n    # Process the hwpx file\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(TEMPLATE_PATH, 'r') as zin:\n        with zipfile.ZipFile(OUTPUT_PATH, 'w', zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zout:\n            for item in zin.infolist():\n                content = zin.read(item.filename)\n\n                #
